- [Clear](#clear) (650): fixed and checked in game, or confirmed not a bug on current master
- [Less certain](#less-certain) (419): fixes checked only partly or with admin tools, unconfirmed results, bugs without a fix
- [Requires more review](#requires-more-review) (188): design choices, behaviour changes, core-code changes, blocked
